    1051           0 : static float distance_to_unit_interval(float x)
    1052             : {
    1053             :   /* The unit interval is between 0 and 1.
    1054             :    * Within the interval, return 0.
    1055             :    * Outside the interval, return the distance to the nearest boundary.
    1056             :    * Intuitively, the function looks like:
    1057             :    *  \ |   | /
    1058             :    * __\|___|/__
    1059             :    *    0   1
    1060             :    */
    1061             : 
    1062           0 :   if (x <= 0.0f) {
    1063           0 :     return -x; /* Distance to left border. */
    1064             :   }
    1065           0 :   if (x <= 1.0f) {
    1066           0 :     return 0.0f; /* Inside unit interval. */
    1067             :   }
    1068           0 :   return x - 1.0f; /* Distance to right border. */
    1069             : }
